# ecosystem.config.cjs作成（CommonJS形式）
cat > ecosystem.config.cjs << 'EOF'
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// .envファイルを読み込んで環境変数オブジェクトに変換
const envPath = path.join(__dirname, '.env');
const envConfig = {};

if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
  const envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8');
  envContent.split('\n').forEach(line => {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
      const [key, ...valueParts] = trimmed.split('=');
      if (key && valueParts.length > 0) {
        envConfig[key.trim()] = valueParts.join('=').trim();
      }
    }
  });
}

module.exports = {
  apps: [{
    name: 'onlyu',
    script: 'dist/index.js',
    env: envConfig,
    instances: 1,
    exec_mode: 'fork',
    autorestart: true,
    watch: false,
    max_memory_restart: '1G'
  }]
};
EOF

# PM2起動
pm2 start ecosystem.config.cjs

# ecosystem.config.cjs作成（CommonJS形式）
cat > ecosystem.config.cjs << 'EOF'
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

// .envファイルを読み込んで環境変数オブジェクトに変換
const envPath = path.join(__dirname, '.env');
const envConfig = {};

if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
  const envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8');
  envContent.split('\n').forEach(line => {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
      const [key, ...valueParts] = trimmed.split('=');
      if (key && valueParts.length > 0) {
        envConfig[key.trim()] = valueParts.join('=').trim();
      }
    }
  });
}

# PM2のecosystem.config.cjsを再作成して環境変数にファイルパスを追加
cat > ecosystem.config.cjs << 'EOF'
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const envPath = path.join(__dirname, '.env');
const envConfig = {
  GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS: path.join(__dirname, 'firebase-service-account.json')
};

if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
  const envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8');
  envContent.split('\n').forEach(line => {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
      const [key, ...valueParts] = trimmed.split('=');
      if (key && valueParts.length > 0) {
        envConfig[key.trim()] = valueParts.join('=').trim();
      }
    }
  });
}

module.exports = {
  apps: [{
    name: 'onlyu',
    script: 'dist/index.js',
    env: envConfig,
    instances: 1,
    exec_mode: 'fork',
    autorestart: true,
    watch: false,
    max_memory_restart: '1G'
  }]
};
EOF

pm2 restart onlyu

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

const envPath = path.join(__dirname, '.env');
const envConfig = {
  GOOGLE_APPLICATION_CREDENTIALS: path.join(__dirname, 'firebase-service-account.json')
};

if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
  const envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, 'utf8');
  envContent.split('\n').forEach(line => {
    const trimmed = line.trim();
    if (trimmed && !trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
      const [key, ...valueParts] = trimmed.split('=');
      if (key && valueParts.length > 0) {
        envConfig[key.trim()] = valueParts.join('=').trim();
      }
    }
  });
}

module.exports = {
  apps: [{
    name: 'onlyu',
    script: 'dist/index.js',
    env: envConfig,
    instances: 1,
    exec_mode: 'fork',
    autorestart: true,
    watch: false,
    max_memory_restart: '1G'
  }]
};

# Nginx設定ファイル作成
sudo tee /etc/nginx/sites-available/onlyu > /dev/null << 'EOF'
server {
    listen 80;
    server_name only-u.fun www.only-u.fun;

    client_max_body_size 500M;

    location / {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:5000;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection 'upgrade';
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
        
        proxy_connect_timeout 600;
        proxy_send_timeout 600;
        proxy_read_timeout 600;
        send_timeout 600;
    }
}
EOF

# シンボリックリンク作成
sudo ln -s /etc/nginx/sites-available/onlyu /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/

# デフォルト設定削除
sudo rm -f /etc/nginx/sites-enabled/default

# Nginx設定テスト
sudo nginx -t

# Nginx再起動
sudo systemctl restart nginx
sudo systemctl enable nginx

# SSL証明書インストール（Let's Encrypt）
sudo apt install certbot python3-certbot-nginx -y

# SSL証明書取得（メールアドレスを入力してください）
sudo certbot --nginx -d only-u.fun -d www.only-u.fun

server {
    server_name only-u.fun www.only-u.fun;
    root /var/www/html;
    # 大きなファイルアップロードを許可（500MB）
    client_max_body_size 500M;
    # ACME challenge location - MUST come BEFORE location /
    location ^~ /.well-known/acme-challenge/ {
        allow all;
        root /var/www/html;
        default_type "text/plain";
        try_files $uri =404;
    }
    # Proxy to Node.js app
    location / {
        proxy_pass http://localhost:5000;
        proxy_http_version 1.1;
        proxy_set_header Upgrade $http_upgrade;
        proxy_set_header Connection 'upgrade';
        proxy_set_header Host $host;
        proxy_set_header X-Real-IP $remote_addr;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
        proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
        proxy_cache_bypass $http_upgrade;
    }
    listen [::]:443 ssl ipv6only=on; # managed by Certbot
    listen 443 ssl; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate /etc/letsencrypt/live/only-u.fun/fullchain.pem; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_certificate_key /etc/letsencrypt/live/only-u.fun/privkey.pem; # managed by Certbot
    include /etc/letsencrypt/options-ssl-nginx.conf; # managed by Certbot
    ssl_dhparam /etc/letsencrypt/ssl-dhparams.pem; # managed by Certbot




}
server {
    listen 80;
    listen [::]:80;
    server_name only-u.fun www.only-u.fun;
    if ($host = www.only-u.fun) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    }
    if ($host = only-u.fun) {
        return 301 https://$host$request_uri;
    }
    return 404; # managed by Certbot
}
